``` math
 & {{\in {\mathbb{R}}^{{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} - 1})} \times {({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} + 1})}}},}
\end{array}
```
``` math
{{{W_{d} = \begin{pmatrix}
1 & 1 & {- 1}
\end{pmatrix}},{W_{d + 1} = \begin{pmatrix}
1
\end{pmatrix} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{1 \times 1}}},{B_{1} = 0}},
```
``` math
{{B_{2} = {0,\ldots}},{{B_{d - 1} = 0},{{B_{d} = {{- K_{d}},\text{and}}}\qquad{B_{d + 1} = 0}}}},
```

let $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, let $`{z_{0} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}},{{z_{1} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} - 1}},{{z_{2} \in {{\mathbb{R}}^{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 2})}} + 1},\ldots}},{{z_{d - 1} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{3}},{{z_{d} \in {\mathbb{R}}},{z_{d + 1} \in {\mathbb{R}}}}}}}`$ satisfy for all $`k \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 1}\}}`$ that $`z_{0} = x`$, $`z_{k} = {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{k}\hspace{0pt}z_{k - 1}} + B_{k}})}}`$, $`z_{d} = {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{d}\hspace{0pt}z_{d - 1}} + B_{d}})}}`$, and

``` math
{z_{d + 1} = {{W_{d + 1}\hspace{0pt}z_{d}} + B_{d + 1}}},
```

and let $`{( \cdot )}^{+}:{{\mathbb{R}}\rightarrow{\lbrack 0,\infty)}}`$ be the function which satisfies for all $`q \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ that $`{(q)}^{+} = {\max{\{ q,0\}}}`$. Note that (374), (467), and (472) imply that $`{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi)}})} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ and

``` math
{z_{d + 1} = {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}}.
```

Next we claim that for all $`k \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 1}\}}`$ it holds that

``` math
{z_{k} = \begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}}\}}} - {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}})}^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+} \\
\vdots \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}}.
```

We now prove (474) by induction on $`k \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 1}\}}`$. For the base case $`k = 1`$ note that (373), (468), (470), and (472) assure that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
z_{1} & {= {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{1}\hspace{0pt}z_{0}} + B_{1}})}} = {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({W_{1}\hspace{0pt}x})}}} \\
 & {{= {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}\begin{pmatrix}
{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}} - {c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}}} \\
{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}} \\
{- {c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}}} \\
{c_{d,3}\hspace{0pt}x_{3}} \\
{- {c_{d,3}\hspace{0pt}x_{3}}} \\
\vdots \\
{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}} \\
{- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}}
\end{pmatrix}} = \begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}}\}}} - {c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}}})}^{+} \\
{({c_{d,3}\hspace{0pt}x_{3}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,3}\hspace{0pt}x_{3}}})}^{+} \\
\vdots \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}}.}
\end{array}
```

This establishes (474) in the base case $`k = 1`$. Next note that item (ii) in Lemma 4.10 implies that for all $`{a,b,c} \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ it holds that

``` math
{{{{({b - a})}^{+} + a} - c} = {{\max{\{ a,b\}}} - c}}.
```

For the induction step $`{\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 2}\}} \ni k\rightarrow{k + 1} \in {\{ 2,3,\ldots,{d - 1}\}}`$ observe that (373), (469), (471), (472), (476) (with $`a = {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}`$, $`b = {\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}}\}}}`$, $`c = {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}`$ in the notation of (476)), and item (i) in Lemma 4.10 demonstrate that for all $`k \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 2}\}}`$ with

``` math
z_{k} = \begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}}\}}} - {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}})}^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+} \\
\vdots \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}
```

it holds that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {z_{k + 1} = {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{k + 1}\hspace{0pt}z_{k}} + B_{k + 1}})}} = {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({W_{k + 1}\hspace{0pt}z_{k}})}}} \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}\begin{pmatrix}
\begin{bmatrix}
\left( {{\max\left\{ {c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}} \right\}} - {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}} \right)^{+} \\
{{{+ \left( {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}} \right)^{+}} - \left( {- {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}} \right)^{+} - \left( {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}} \right)^{+}} + \left( {- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}} \right)^{+}}
\end{bmatrix} \\
{{({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+} - {({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+}} \\
{{- {({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+}} + {({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+}} \\
{{({c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}})}^{+} - {({- {c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}}})}^{+}} \\
{{- {({c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}})}^{+}} + {({- {c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}}})}^{+}} \\
\vdots \\
{{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} - {({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}} \\
{{- {({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+}} + {({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}}
\end{pmatrix}}} \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}\begin{pmatrix}
\begin{matrix}
{{\left( {{\max\left\{ {c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}} \right\}} - {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}} \right)^{+} + {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}} - {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}}
\end{matrix} \\
{c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}} \\
{- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}} \\
{c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}} \\
{- {c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}}} \\
\vdots \\
{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}} \\
{- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}}
\end{pmatrix}}} \\
 & {{= \begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}},{c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}\}}} - {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}}})}^{+} \\
\vdots \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}}.}
\end{array}
```

Induction thus proves (474). Next observe that (470), (471), (472), (474), and item (ii) in Lemma 4.10 imply that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
z_{d} & {= {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{d}\hspace{0pt}z_{d - 1}} + B_{d}})}}} \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\begin{pmatrix}
1 & 1 & {- 1}
\end{pmatrix}\hspace{0pt}\begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,{d - 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{d - 1}}\}}} - {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}} - K_{d}} \right)}} \\
 & {= \mathbf{A}_{1}\left( \left( \max{\{ c_{d,1}x_{1},\ldots,c_{d,{d - 1}}x_{d - 1}\}} - c_{d,d}x_{d} \right)^{+} \right.} \\
 & \left. + {(c_{d,d}x_{d})}^{+} - {( - c_{d,d}x_{d})}^{+} - K_{d} \right) \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\left( {{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,{d - 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{d - 1}}\}}} - {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}} \right)^{+} + {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}} - K_{d}} \right)}} \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,{d - 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{d - 1}},{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}} \right)}} \\
 & {{= {\max{\{{{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with (470)–(473) establishes that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} & {= z_{d + 1} = {{W_{d + 1}\hspace{0pt}z_{d}} + B_{d + 1}}} \\
 & {{= z_{d} = {\max{\{{{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}}.}
\end{array}
```

In addition, observe that Lemma 4.11 implies that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi)}} & {= {{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1})}\hspace{0pt}{({d + 1})}}} \\
 & {{+ \left\lbrack {\sum_{k = 1}^{d - 1}{{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1})}\hspace{0pt}{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} + 1 + 1})}}} \right\rbrack} + {1\hspace{0pt}{({1 + 1})}}} \\
 & {{\leq {6\hspace{0pt}d^{3}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this, (473), and (480) completes the proof of Lemma 4.12. ∎

###### Proposition 4.13.

Assume Setting 4.1 and let $`{{(K_{d})}_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{(c_{d,i})}_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}}} \subseteq {\lbrack 0,\infty)}`$ satisfy that $`{{\sup_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}}c_{d,i}} < \infty}.`$ Then

1.  (i)
    there exist unique continuous functions $`u_{d}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}}`$, $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that $`{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(T,x)}} = {\max{\{{{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}`$, which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{\inf_{q \in {(0,\infty)}}{\sup_{{(t,x)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}}\frac{|{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}|}{1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{q}}}} < \infty`$, and which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{u_{d}|}_{{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ is a viscosity solution of

    ``` math
    \begin{array}{cl}
     & {{{({\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\hspace{0pt}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} + \left\langle {{({\nabla_{x}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}},{\mu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right\rangle_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}} \\
     & {{+ {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}{{Trace}\left( {\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}^{\ast}\hspace{0pt}{({{Hess}_{x}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \right)}}} = 0}
    \end{array}
    ```
    for $`{(t,x)} \in {{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ and
2.  (ii)
    there exist $`{\mathfrak{C}} \in {(0,\infty)}`$, $`{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}}} \subseteq \mathcal{N}`$ such that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 4}}`$, $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 2}}`$, $`{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$, and

    ``` math
    {\left\lbrack {\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}{\left| {{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right|^{p}\hspace{0pt}\nu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{({d\hspace{0pt}x})}}} \right\rbrack^{1/p} \leq \varepsilon}.
    ```

###### Proof of Proposition 4.13.

Throughout this proof let $`\varphi_{d}:{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}}`$, $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that

``` math
{{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{{{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}},
```

let $`{{(\chi_{d})}_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{(\phi_{d,\delta})}_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}}}} \subseteq \mathcal{N}`$ satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}} \leq {6\hspace{0pt}d^{3}}`$, $`{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$, $`{{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}`$ (cf. Lemma 4.12), and $`\phi_{d,\delta} = \chi_{d}`$, and let $`C \in {\lbrack 0,\infty)}`$ be given by $`C = {\sup_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}}c_{d,i}}`$. Note that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that

``` math
{{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d,\delta})}} = {\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}} = \varphi_{d} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}}.
```

This and the fact that for all $`{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}`$ it holds that $`c_{d,i} \in {\lbrack 0,\infty)}`$ ensures that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
{|{{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d,\delta})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}|} & {= {|{{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}|} = {|{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}|}} \\
 & {= {\max{\{{{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}} \\
 & {\leq {\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}{|x_{1}|}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}{|x_{2}|}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}{|x_{d}|}}\}}}} \\
 & {\leq {C\hspace{0pt}{\max{\{{|x_{1}|},{|x_{2}|},\ldots,{|x_{d}|}\}}}}} \\
 & {{\leq {C\hspace{0pt}\left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}} \leq {C\hspace{0pt}d^{0}\hspace{0pt}{({1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{2}})}}}.}
\end{array}
```

In addition, observe that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that

``` math
{{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d,\delta})}} = {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}} \leq {6\hspace{0pt}d^{3}} = {6\hspace{0pt}d^{3}\hspace{0pt}\delta^{- 0}}}.
```

Furthermore, note that (485) ensures that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
\left| {{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d,\delta})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right| & {= \left| {{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right|} \\
 & {{= \left| {{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} - {\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right| = 0 \leq {d^{0}\hspace{0pt}\delta^{0}\hspace{0pt}{({1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{2}})}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this, (485), (486), (487), the fact that $`{(\varphi_{d})}_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}}`$ are continuous functions, the hypothesis that for all $`q \in {(0,\infty)}`$ it holds that

``` math
{{\sup\limits_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}}\left\lbrack {d^{- {\theta\hspace{0pt}q}}\hspace{0pt}{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}{{\| x\|}_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{q}\hspace{0pt}\nu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{({d\hspace{0pt}x})}}}} \right\rbrack} < \infty},
```

and Lemma 4.2 with Theorem 3.14 (with $`T = T`$, $`r = 1`$, $`R = 1`$, $`v = 0`$, $`w = 0`$, $`z = 3`$, $`\mathbf{z} = 0`$, $`\theta = \theta`$, $`{\mathfrak{c}} = {\max{\{ 6,C,{2\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {\sup_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}}{({{|\alpha_{d,i}|} + {|\beta_{d,i}|}})}} \right\rbrack}\}}}`$, $`\mathbf{v} = 2`$, $`p = p`$, $`\nu_{d} = \nu_{d}`$, $`\varphi_{d} = \varphi_{d}`$, $`\mu_{d} = \mu_{d}`$, $`\sigma_{d} = \sigma_{d}`$, $`{a\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{ x,0\}}}`$, $`\phi_{d,\delta} = \phi_{d,\delta}`$ for $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ in the notation of Theorem 3.14) demonstrates that there exist unique continuous functions $`v_{d}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}}`$, $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that $`{v_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x)}} = {\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}`$, which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{\inf_{q \in {(0,\infty)}}{\sup_{{(t,x)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}}\frac{|{v_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}|}{1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{q}}}} < \infty`$, and which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{v_{d}|}_{{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ is a viscosity solution of

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{({\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\hspace{0pt}v_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} & {= {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}{{Trace}\left( {\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}^{\ast}\hspace{0pt}{({{Hess}_{x}v_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \right)}}} \\
 & {+ \left\langle {{({\nabla_{x}v_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}},{\mu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right\rangle_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}}
\end{array}
```

for $`{(t,x)} \in {{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ and that there exist $`{\mathfrak{C}} \in {(0,\infty)}`$, $`{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}}} \subseteq \mathcal{N}`$ such that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 4}}`$, $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 2}}`$, $`{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$, and

``` math
{\left\lbrack {\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}{\left| {{v_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(T,x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right|^{p}\hspace{0pt}\nu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{({d\hspace{0pt}x})}}} \right\rbrack^{1/p} \leq \varepsilon}.
```

Corollary 4.5 hence assures that there exist unique continuous functions $`u_{d}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}}`$, $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, which satisfy that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ it holds that

``` math
\begin{aligned}
{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(T,x)}} & {{= {\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{{{\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}},}
\end{aligned}
```

which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{\inf_{q \in {(0,\infty)}}{\sup_{{(t,x)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}}\frac{|{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}|}{1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{q}}}} < \infty`$, and which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{u_{d}|}_{{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ is a viscosity solution of

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{{({\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\hspace{0pt}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} + \left\langle {{({\nabla_{x}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}},{\mu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right\rangle_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}} \\
 & {{+ {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}{{Trace}\left( {\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}^{\ast}\hspace{0pt}{({{Hess}_{x}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \right)}}} = 0}
\end{array}
```

for $`{(t,x)} \in {{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ and that it holds for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{\left\lbrack {\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}{\left| {{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right|^{p}\hspace{0pt}\nu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{({d\hspace{0pt}x})}}} \right\rbrack^{1/p} \leq \varepsilon}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with the fact that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 4}}`$, $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 2}}`$, and $`{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ establishes items (i)–(ii). The proof of Proposition 4.13 is thus completed. ∎

### 4.6 Artificial neural network approximations for call on min options

In this subsection we establish in Proposition 4.15 below that ANN approximations overcome the curse of dimensionality in the numerical approximation of the Black-Scholes model in the case of call on min options. Our proof of Proposition 4.15 employs the ANN representation result for the payoff functions associated to call on min options in Lemma 4.14 below.

###### Lemma 4.14.

Assume Setting 4.1 and let $`{{(K_{d})}_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{(c_{d,i})}_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}}} \subseteq {\mathbb{R}}`$. Then there exists $`{(\phi_{d})}_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}} \subseteq \mathcal{N}`$ such that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d})}} \leq {6\hspace{0pt}d^{3}}`$, $`{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$, and

``` math
{{{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{{{\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}}.
```

###### Proof of Lemma 4.14.

Throughout this proof let $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, let

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
\phi & {= {({(W_{1},B_{1})},{(W_{2},B_{2})},\ldots,{(W_{d},B_{d})},{(W_{d + 1},B_{d + 1})})}} \\
 & {\in {({{\mathbb{R}}^{{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1})} \times d} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1}})}} \\
 & {\times {( \times_{k = 1}^{d - 1}{({\mathbb{R}}^{{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} - 1})} \times {({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} + 1})}} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} - 1})})}} \\
 & {\times {({{\mathbb{R}}^{1 \times 1} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{1}})}}
\end{array}
```

(i.e., $`\phi`$ corresponds to fully connected feedforward artificial neural network with $`d + 2`$ layers with dimensions $`(d,{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1},{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 2})}} + 1},{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 3})}} + 1},\ldots,3,1,1)`$) satisfy for all $`k \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 2}\}}`$ that

``` math
{W_{1} = \begin{pmatrix}
{- c_{d,1}} & c_{d,2} & 0 & \cdots & 0 \\
0 & c_{d,2} & 0 & \cdots & 0 \\
0 & {- c_{d,2}} & 0 & \cdots & 0 \\
0 & 0 & c_{d,3} & \cdots & 0 \\
0 & 0 & {- c_{d,3}} & \cdots & 0 \\
\vdots & \vdots & \vdots & \ddots & \vdots \\
0 & 0 & 0 & \cdots & c_{d,d} \\
0 & 0 & 0 & \cdots & {- c_{d,d}}
\end{pmatrix} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1})} \times d}},
```
``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
W_{k + 1} & {= \begin{pmatrix}
1 & {- 1} & 1 & 1 & {- 1} & 0 & 0 & \ldots & 0 & 0 \\
0 & 0 & 0 & 1 & {- 1} & 0 & 0 & \ldots & 0 & 0 \\
0 & 0 & 0 & {- 1} & 1 & 0 & 0 & \ldots & 0 & 0 \\
0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 1 & {- 1} & \ldots & 0 & 0 \\
0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & {- 1} & 1 & \ldots & 0 & 0 \\
\vdots & \vdots & \vdots & \vdots & \vdots & \vdots & \vdots & \ddots & \vdots & \vdots \\
0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & \ldots & 1 & {- 1} \\
0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 & \ldots & {- 1} & 1
\end{pmatrix}} \\
 & {{\in {\mathbb{R}}^{{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} - 1})} \times {({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} + 1})}}},}
\end{array}
```
``` math
{{{W_{d} = \begin{pmatrix}
{- 1} & 1 & {- 1}
\end{pmatrix}},{W_{d + 1} = \begin{pmatrix}
1
\end{pmatrix} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{1 \times 1}}},{B_{1} = 0}},
```
``` math
{{B_{2} = {0,\ldots}},{{B_{d - 1} = 0},{{B_{d} = {{- K_{d}},\text{and}}}\qquad{B_{d + 1} = 0}}}},
```

let $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, let $`{z_{0} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}},{{z_{1} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} - 1}},{{z_{2} \in {{\mathbb{R}}^{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 2})}} + 1},\ldots}},{{z_{d - 1} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{3}},{{z_{d} \in {\mathbb{R}}},{z_{d + 1} \in {\mathbb{R}}}}}}}`$ satisfy for all $`k \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 1}\}}`$ that $`z_{0} = x`$, $`z_{k} = {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{k}\hspace{0pt}z_{k - 1}} + B_{k}})}}`$, $`z_{d} = {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{d}\hspace{0pt}z_{d - 1}} + B_{d}})}}`$, and

``` math
{z_{d + 1} = {{W_{d + 1}\hspace{0pt}z_{d}} + B_{d + 1}}},
```

and let $`{( \cdot )}^{+}:{{\mathbb{R}}\rightarrow{\lbrack 0,\infty)}}`$ be the function which satisfies for all $`q \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ that $`{(q)}^{+} = {\max{\{ q,0\}}}`$. Note that (374), (496), and (501) imply that $`{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi)}})} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ and

``` math
{z_{d + 1} = {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}}.
```

Next we claim that for all $`k \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 1}\}}`$ it holds that

``` math
{z_{k} = \begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}} - {\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}}\}}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}})}^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+} \\
\vdots \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}}.
```

We now prove (503) by induction on $`k \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 1}\}}`$. For the base case $`k = 1`$ note that (373), (497), (499), and (501) assure that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
z_{1} & {= {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{1}\hspace{0pt}z_{0}} + B_{1}})}} = {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({W_{1}\hspace{0pt}x})}}} \\
 & {{= {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}\begin{pmatrix}
{{- {c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}}} + {c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}}} \\
{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}} \\
{- {c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}}} \\
{c_{d,3}\hspace{0pt}x_{3}} \\
{- {c_{d,3}\hspace{0pt}x_{3}}} \\
\vdots \\
{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}} \\
{- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}}
\end{pmatrix}} = \begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}} - {\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}}\}}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}}})}^{+} \\
{({c_{d,3}\hspace{0pt}x_{3}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,3}\hspace{0pt}x_{3}}})}^{+} \\
\vdots \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}}.}
\end{array}
```

This establishes (503) in the base case $`k = 1`$. Next note that item (iii) in Lemma 4.10 implies that for all $`{a,b,c} \in {\mathbb{R}}`$ it holds that

``` math
{{{{({a - b})}^{+} - a} + c} = {c - {\min{\{ a,b\}}}}}.
```

For the induction step $`{\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 2}\}} \ni k\rightarrow{k + 1} \in {\{ 2,3,\ldots,{d - 1}\}}`$ observe that (373), (498), (500), (501), (505) (with $`a = {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}`$, $`b = {\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}}\}}}`$, $`c = {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}`$ in the notation of (505)), and item (i) in Lemma 4.10 demonstrate that for all $`k \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,{d - 2}\}}`$ with

``` math
z_{k} = \begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}} - {\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}}\}}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}})}^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+} \\
\vdots \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}
```

it holds that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {z_{k + 1} = {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{k + 1}\hspace{0pt}z_{k}} + B_{k + 1}})}} = {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}{({W_{k + 1}\hspace{0pt}z_{k}})}}} \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}\begin{pmatrix}
\begin{bmatrix}
\left( {{c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}} - {\min\left\{ {c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}} \right\}}} \right)^{+} \\
{{{- \left( {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}} \right)^{+}} + \left( {- {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}} \right)^{+} + \left( {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}} \right)^{+}} - \left( {- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}} \right)^{+}}
\end{bmatrix} \\
{{({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+} - {({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+}} \\
{{- {({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+}} + {({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+}} \\
{{({c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}})}^{+} - {({- {c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}}})}^{+}} \\
{{- {({c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}})}^{+}} + {({- {c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}}})}^{+}} \\
\vdots \\
{{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} - {({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}} \\
{{- {({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+}} + {({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}}
\end{pmatrix}}} \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1}\hspace{0pt}\begin{pmatrix}
\begin{matrix}
{{\left( {{c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}} - {\min\left\{ {c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}} \right\}}} \right)^{+} - {c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}} + {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}}
\end{matrix} \\
{c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}} \\
{- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}} \\
{c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}} \\
{- {c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}}} \\
\vdots \\
{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}} \\
{- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}}
\end{pmatrix}}} \\
 & {{= \begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}} - {\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,k}\hspace{0pt}x_{k}},{c_{d,{k + 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 1}}\}}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 2}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 2}}})}^{+} \\
{({c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,{k + 3}}\hspace{0pt}x_{k + 3}}})}^{+} \\
\vdots \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}}.}
\end{array}
```

Induction thus proves (503). Next observe that (498), (500), (501), (503), and item (iii) in Lemma 4.10 imply that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
z_{d} & {= {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}{({{W_{d}\hspace{0pt}z_{d - 1}} + B_{d}})}}} \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\begin{pmatrix}
{- 1} & 1 & {- 1}
\end{pmatrix}\hspace{0pt}\begin{pmatrix}
\left( {{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}} - {\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,{d - 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{d - 1}}\}}}} \right)^{+} \\
{({c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}})}^{+} \\
{({- {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}})}^{+}
\end{pmatrix}} - K_{d}} \right)}} \\
 & {= \mathbf{A}_{1}\left( - \left( c_{d,d}x_{d} - \min{\{ c_{d,1}x_{1},\ldots,c_{d,{d - 1}}x_{d - 1}\}} \right)^{+} \right.} \\
 & \left. + {(c_{d,d}x_{d})}^{+} - {( - c_{d,d}x_{d})}^{+} - K_{d} \right) \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{{- \left( {{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}} - {\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,{d - 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{d - 1}}\}}}} \right)^{+}} + {c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}} - K_{d}} \right)}} \\
 & {= {\mathbf{A}_{1}\hspace{0pt}\left( {{\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,{d - 1}}\hspace{0pt}x_{d - 1}},{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}} \right)}} \\
 & {{= {\max{\{{{\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with (499)–(502) establishes that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi)}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} & {= z_{d + 1} = {{W_{d + 1}\hspace{0pt}z_{d}} + B_{d + 1}}} \\
 & {{= z_{d} = {\max{\{{{\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}}.}
\end{array}
```

In addition, observe that Lemma 4.11 implies that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi)}} & {= {{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - 1})}} + 1})}\hspace{0pt}{({d + 1})}}} \\
 & {{+ \left\lbrack {\sum_{k = 1}^{d - 1}{{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - {({k + 1})}})}} + 1})}\hspace{0pt}{({{2\hspace{0pt}{({d - k})}} + 1 + 1})}}} \right\rbrack} + {1\hspace{0pt}{({1 + 1})}}} \\
 & {{\leq {6\hspace{0pt}d^{3}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this, (502), and (509) completes the proof of Lemma 4.14. ∎

###### Proposition 4.15.

Assume Setting 4.1 and let $`{{(K_{d})}_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{(c_{d,i})}_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}}} \subseteq {\lbrack 0,\infty)}`$ satisfy that $`{{\sup_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}}c_{d,i}} < \infty}.`$ Then

1.  (i)
    there exist unique continuous functions $`u_{d}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}}`$, $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that $`{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(T,x)}} = {\max{\{{{\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}`$, which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{\inf_{q \in {(0,\infty)}}{\sup_{{(t,x)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}}\frac{|{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}|}{1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{q}}}} < \infty`$, and which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{u_{d}|}_{{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ is a viscosity solution of

    ``` math
    \begin{array}{cl}
     & {{{({\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\hspace{0pt}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} + \left\langle {{({\nabla_{x}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}},{\mu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right\rangle_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}} \\
     & {{+ {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}{{Trace}\left( {\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}^{\ast}\hspace{0pt}{({{Hess}_{x}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \right)}}} = 0}
    \end{array}
    ```
    for $`{(t,x)} \in {{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ and
2.  (ii)
    there exist $`{\mathfrak{C}} \in {(0,\infty)}`$, $`{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}}} \subseteq \mathcal{N}`$ such that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 4}}`$, $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 2}}`$, $`{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$, and

    ``` math
    {\left\lbrack {\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}{\left| {{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right|^{p}\hspace{0pt}\nu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{({d\hspace{0pt}x})}}} \right\rbrack^{1/p} \leq \varepsilon}.
    ```

###### Proof of Proposition 4.15.

Throughout this proof let $`\varphi_{d}:{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}}`$, $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that

``` math
{{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{{{\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}},
```

let $`{{(\chi_{d})}_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{(\phi_{d,\delta})}_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}}}} \subseteq \mathcal{N}`$ satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}} \leq {6\hspace{0pt}d^{3}}`$, $`{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$, $`{{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}`$ (cf. Lemma 4.14), and $`\phi_{d,\delta} = \chi_{d}`$, and let $`C \in {\lbrack 0,\infty)}`$ be given by $`C = {\sup_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}}c_{d,i}}`$. Note that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that

``` math
{{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d,\delta})}} = {\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}} = \varphi_{d} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}}.
```

This ensures that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
{|{{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d,\delta})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}|} & {= {|{{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}|} = {|{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}|}} \\
 & {= {\max{\{{{\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}} \\
 & {\leq {\max{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}{|x_{1}|}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}{|x_{2}|}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}{|x_{d}|}}\}}}} \\
 & {\leq {C\hspace{0pt}{\max{\{{|x_{1}|},{|x_{2}|},\ldots,{|x_{d}|}\}}}}} \\
 & {{\leq {C\hspace{0pt}\left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}} \leq {C\hspace{0pt}d^{0}\hspace{0pt}{({1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{2}})}}}.}
\end{array}
```

In addition, observe that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that

``` math
{{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d,\delta})}} = {\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}} \leq {6\hspace{0pt}d^{3}} = {6\hspace{0pt}d^{3}\hspace{0pt}\delta^{- 0}}}.
```

Furthermore, note that (514) ensures that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
\left| {{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\phi_{d,\delta})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right| & {= \left| {{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\chi_{d})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right|} \\
 & {{= \left| {{\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} - {\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right| = 0 \leq {d^{0}\hspace{0pt}\delta^{0}\hspace{0pt}{({1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{2}})}}}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this, (514), (515), (516), the fact that $`{(\varphi_{d})}_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}}`$ are continuous functions, the hypothesis that for all $`q \in {(0,\infty)}`$ it holds that

``` math
{{\sup\limits_{d \in {\mathbb{N}}}\left\lbrack {d^{- {\theta\hspace{0pt}q}}\hspace{0pt}{\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}{{\| x\|}_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{q}\hspace{0pt}\nu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{({d\hspace{0pt}x})}}}} \right\rbrack} < \infty},
```

and Lemma 4.2 with Theorem 3.14 (with $`T = T`$, $`r = 1`$, $`R = 1`$, $`v = 0`$, $`w = 0`$, $`z = 3`$, $`\mathbf{z} = 0`$, $`\theta = \theta`$, $`{\mathfrak{c}} = {\max{\{ 6,C,{2\hspace{0pt}\left\lbrack {\sup_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{i \in {\{ 1,2,\ldots,d\}}}}{({{|\alpha_{d,i}|} + {|\beta_{d,i}|}})}} \right\rbrack}\}}}`$, $`\mathbf{v} = 2`$, $`p = p`$, $`\nu_{d} = \nu_{d}`$, $`\varphi_{d} = \varphi_{d}`$, $`\mu_{d} = \mu_{d}`$, $`\sigma_{d} = \sigma_{d}`$, $`{a\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{ x,0\}}}`$, $`\phi_{d,\delta} = \phi_{d,\delta}`$ for $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}`$, $`\delta \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ in the notation of Theorem 3.14) demonstrates that there exist unique continuous functions $`v_{d}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}}`$, $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ that $`{v_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x)}} = {\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}`$, which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{\inf_{q \in {(0,\infty)}}{\sup_{{(t,x)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}}\frac{|{v_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}|}{1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{q}}}} < \infty`$, and which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{v_{d}|}_{{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ is a viscosity solution of

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
{{({\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\hspace{0pt}v_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} & {= {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}{{Trace}\left( {\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}^{\ast}\hspace{0pt}{({{Hess}_{x}v_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \right)}}} \\
 & {+ \left\langle {{({\nabla_{x}v_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}},{\mu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right\rangle_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}}
\end{array}
```

for $`{(t,x)} \in {{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ and that there exist $`{\mathfrak{C}} \in {(0,\infty)}`$, $`{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}_{{d \in {\mathbb{N}}},{\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}}} \subseteq \mathcal{N}`$ such that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 4}}`$, $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 2}}`$, $`{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$, and

``` math
{\left\lbrack {\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}{\left| {{v_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(T,x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right|^{p}\hspace{0pt}\nu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{({d\hspace{0pt}x})}}} \right\rbrack^{1/p} \leq \varepsilon}.
```

Corollary 4.5 hence assures that there exist unique continuous functions $`u_{d}:{{{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}\rightarrow{\mathbb{R}}}`$, $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, which satisfy that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`x = {(x_{1},x_{2},\ldots,x_{d})} \in {\mathbb{R}}^{d}`$ it holds that

``` math
\begin{aligned}
{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(T,x)}} & {{= {\varphi_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} = {\max{\{{{\min{\{{c_{d,1}\hspace{0pt}x_{1}},{c_{d,2}\hspace{0pt}x_{2}},\ldots,{c_{d,d}\hspace{0pt}x_{d}}\}}} - K_{d}},0\}}}},}
\end{aligned}
```

which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{\inf_{q \in {(0,\infty)}}{\sup_{{(t,x)} \in {{\lbrack 0,T\rbrack} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}}\frac{|{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}}|}{1 + \left\| x \right\|_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}^{q}}}} < \infty`$, and which satisfy for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$ that $`{u_{d}|}_{{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ is a viscosity solution of

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{{({\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\hspace{0pt}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} + \left\langle {{({\nabla_{x}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}},{\mu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}} \right\rangle_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}} \\
 & {{+ {\frac{1}{2}\hspace{0pt}{{Trace}\left( {\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}\hspace{0pt}{\lbrack{\sigma_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}\rbrack}^{\ast}\hspace{0pt}{({{Hess}_{x}u_{d}})}\hspace{0pt}{(t,x)}} \right)}}} = 0}
\end{array}
```

for $`{(t,x)} \in {{(0,T)} \times {\mathbb{R}}^{d}}`$ and that it holds for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ that

``` math
\begin{array}{cl}
 & {{\left\lbrack {\int_{{\mathbb{R}}^{d}}{\left| {{u_{d}\hspace{0pt}{(0,x)}} - {{({\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}})}\hspace{0pt}{(x)}}} \right|^{p}\hspace{0pt}\nu_{d}\hspace{0pt}{({d\hspace{0pt}x})}}} \right\rbrack^{1/p} \leq \varepsilon}.}
\end{array}
```

Combining this with the fact that for all $`d \in {\mathbb{N}}`$, $`\varepsilon \in {(0,1\rbrack}`$ it holds that $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 4}}`$, $`{\mathcal{P}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \leq {{\mathfrak{C}}\hspace{0pt}d^{{5\hspace{0pt}\theta} + 3}\hspace{0pt}\varepsilon^{- 2}}`$, and $`{\mathcal{R}\hspace{0pt}{(\psi_{d,\varepsilon})}} \in {C\hspace{0pt}{({\mathbb{R}}^{d},{\mathbb{R}})}}`$ establishes items (i)–(ii). The proof of Proposition 4.15 is thus completed. ∎
